Notify only after successful SQL\n\t// commit; an authoritative FULL barrier may also retire earlier requests.\n\t// Neither path may report failure after the commit has already succeeded.\n\tvoid OnCommit(uint64_t appended, bool full, std::pair<int64_t, int64_t> range) noexcept {\n\t\ttry {\n\t\t\tbool notify;\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tstd::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);\n\t\t\t\trequested = std::max(requested, appended);\n\t\t\t\tif (full) {\n\t\t\t\t\tcompleted = std::max(completed, appended);\n\t\t\t\t\tpending = {};\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tMerge(range);\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tnotify = full || requested - completed >= START_BYTES;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif (notify)\n\t\t\t\twake.notify_one();\n\t\t} catch (...) {\n\t\t\terror.store(true);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\nprivate:\n\tvoid Merge(std::pair<int64_t, int64_t> range) {\n\t\tif (range.second <= range.first)\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\tpending.first = pending.second ? std::min(pending.first, range.first) : range.first;\n\t\tpending.second = std::max(pending.second, range.second);\n\t}\n\tvoid Run() {\n\t\tstd::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(mutex);\n\t\twhile (true) {\n\t\t\twake.wait(lock, [this] { return stopped || requested - completed >= START_BYTES; });\n\t\t\tif (stopped)\n\t\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t\tauto target = requested;\n\t\t\tlock.unlock();\n\t\t\tbool success = false;\n\t\t\tstd::pair<int64_t, int64_t> range {};\n\t\t\ttry {\n\t\t\t\t// This separate open-file description must not share flock state\n\t\t\t\t// with writer transactions. SH permits appends and excludes GC.\n\t\t\t\tPayloadFile::Guard lease(*file, false);\n\t\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\t\tstd::lock_guard<std::mutex> state(mutex);\n\t\t\t\t\t// GC or another FULL barrier may have retired this request\n\t\t\t\t\t// while the worker waited for the payload lease.\n\t\t\t\t\tif (stopped)\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t\t\t\tif (completed >= target) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tsuccess = true;\n\t\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\t\ttarget = requested;\n\t\t\t\t\t\trange = std::exchange(pending, {});\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif (!success) {\n\t\t\t\t\tfile->WritebackRange(range);\n\t\t\t\t\tsuccess = true;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} catch (const Error &failure) {\n\t\t\t\tif (failure.code != ErrorCode::Busy)\n\t\t\t\t\terror.store(true);\n\t\t\t} catch (...) {\n\t\t\t\terror.store(true);\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlock.lock();\n\t\t\tif (success)\n\t\t\t\tcompleted = std::max(completed, target);\n\t\t\telse {\n\t\t\t\t// Retry the claimed range together with later commits, unless a\n\t\t\t\t// successful FULL barrier already retired the captured target.\n\t\t\t\tif (completed < target)\n\t\t\t\t\tMerge(range);\n\t\t\t\twake.wait_for(lock, std::chrono::milliseconds(20), [this] { return stopped; });\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tstd::unique_ptr<PayloadFile> file;\n\tstd::mutex mutex;\n\tstd::condition_variable wake;\n\tstd::thread worker;\n\tstd::atomic<bool> error {false};\n\t// completed means submitted (or retired by FULL), never durable.\n\tuint64_t requested = 0, completed = 0;\n\tstd::pair<int64_t, int64_t> pending {};\n\tbool stopped = false;\n};\n} // namespace vane_fs\n",
